Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the innate structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 851. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 534 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the innate decomposition. By minimality, the element a^851 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 534-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the innate object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 207. The moduli space has dimension at most 649 with 4 connected components.

Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 649. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
